We can write a "coordinate descent with active peaks and line search" that is likely to polish:
- At current a, compute b and M.
- Determine active peaks: all k with b_k ≥ M - δ. δ maybe M * 1e-4 or absolute 1e-8. Let P weights proportional to slack? Use log-sum returned p.
- Compute pressure H = 2*corr(P,a) (grad of weighted M). For mass transfer i->j, approximate ΔM ≈ H_j - H_i (if moving δ from i to j? Actually if a_i decreases, a_j increases: derivative = H_j - H_i). We want H_j < H_i.
- Choose top i by H, bottom j by H. For robust, generate candidates:
  * For each of top 20 high H (with a_i>eps), choose 10 low H destinations (excluding active inverse?), try moving 1% to 20%.
  * Also for each top active peak k, choose pair (i,k-i) with largest product; try reducing larger coordinate and moving to low H dest.
- Evaluate exact M; accept if M < best - tol*... Use scaled threshold 1e-12.
- If no improvement after many tries, use "smoothing": a ← a + ε (projection noise) and accept/reject with simulated annealing.

This pair CD may be slow due many FFT evals. We can limit.

Could approximate M quickly via only active peaks. For candidate move, instead of full FFT, compute changes to top active b exactly in O(num_top * ?). Actually e_i*a term linear; plus δ² e_i*e_i at k=2i, e_j*e_j at k=2j, cross -2δ² e_i e_j at k=i+j). We can compute for top K peaks in O(K) per candidate by indexing a. This is much faster than FFT! This can be used for line search/screening many moves. Then accept best and verify full FFT occasionally. Good.

Let's derive exact update for a' = a + u where u_i=-δ, u_j=+δ, others 0. Convolution b' = b + 2? Need linear and quadratic:
b_k = ∑ a_l a_{k-l}.
Add u: b'_k = b_k + ∑ (u_l a_{k-l} + a_l u_{k-l}) + ∑ u_l u_{k-l}.
Linear: l=i: u_i a_{k-i} = -δ a_{k-i}; term a_l u_{k-l}: k-l=i => l=k-i, u_i a_{k-i} = -δ a_{k-i}. Total linear -2δ a_{k-i}. For j: +2δ a_{k-j}. Quadratic: u_i u_{k-i}: k=2i -> δ²? u_i² = δ² at k=2i. u_j u_{k-j}: k=2j -> δ². Cross u_i u_{j} + u_j u_i if k=i+j: when k=i+j, u_i u_j and u_j u_i, each (-δ)(+δ)=-δ², two => -2δ². Also if i=j none but distinct. So b'_k = b_k + 2δ(a_{k-j}-a_{k-i}) + δ²[ 1_{2i=k}+1_{2j=k} -2 1_{i+j=k} ]. Let's verify using derivative for when i,j distinct. Good. If i==j no.

Alternatively, we can use full FFT for each promising candidate (top 5) and efficient approximate for all. This can speed pair CD dramatically compared to previous.

Let's design "efficient pair_cd2":
Input a sum1, time limit.
Maintain b=autocorr(a) exact, M,max idx.
Loop until time:
1. Compute top peaks: T= min(200, n?), indices sorted by b descending. Active threshold. P uniform over top? Or weights maybe proportional to b (softmax). Use top 50.
2. Compute pressure H = 2∑_{k∈S} w_k a_{k-j}. Use FFT maybe each iteration. Could also compute H for top peaks by summing O(T*n) too slow. Use FFT: p length 2n-1 with w; conv p with a_rev to get H (as previous). One FFT cost.
3. Generate candidate moves:
   - High source indices: top 30 by H, with a[i]>min_eps.
   - Low dest indices: bottom 30 by H, maybe plus random 100 low pressure.
   For each src i, dst j in combinations (maybe 30*30=900), try δ fractions [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,a_i]? δ≤a_i.
   Approx new M for move: compute for active peaks only (top 100) using formula. Also include k=i,j,2i,2j,i+j if in range. If approx < current_M*(1 - 1e-8), candidate.
   But since H is gradient for active weights, low H dest likely good. Rank by approximate decrease; evaluate exact full FFT for top N (e.g., 3) candidates with full b. Accept best if M_full < M*(1-1e-10). Update a,b,M.
4. If no exact improvement after batch, do random exploration: perturb a with small Gaussian projected to simplex, compute full b, accept if improves or with simulated annealing prob exp(-Δ/T). Use T decreasing. Or use "jump" by applying a small steepest-step: a = normalize(clip(a - step*H + noise,0)), full eval.
5. Periodically recompute full b and top.

This local search can polish. Need avoid too many full FFTs in batch. But full FFT maybe cheap. We can evaluate exact for e.g. 50 candidates per pressure recompute. Each candidate full FFT one step. Pressure recompute every 10-20 candidates. Good.

Let's detail helper functions.

fast_autocorr(a): as previous, but optimize: use np.fft.rfft/a length. Need ensure full length 2n-1. We can cache FFT? For each step, compute b. In pair_cd, after evaluating candidate, need b. We can implement function that returns b, max.

For Nesterov/multiplicative: compute grad pressure.
Function compute_pressure(p, a, fft_size, A=None): returns H length n. p is length 2n-1 (or 0 except top). Need H[j] = ∑_k p[k] a[k-j] (valid). We derived Conv = irfft(fft(p) * fft(a_rev)), H=Conv[n-1:2n-1]. Let's verify with small example to avoid shift bug. a length n, p length 2n-1. def mul_update(a, beta, top_k, eta, t0, time, verbose):
 n=len(a); best=a; b=M; old=M;
 while time:
   b=M; sort top_idx = argsort(b)[-top_k]; weights softmax(beta*(b[top]-max)); normalize.
   H=pressure_peaks(top_idx, weights, a)
   # H has units? grad=2H; but for update scale not matter.
   # Need choose η. H ~? For a sum1, b~1/n, H~? For active peak p weight, H_j ~2 p * a_{k-j} ~ few/n. η maybe 10? In log-sum-exp smoothed gradient with 1/beta, eta=0.1/(beta...) small. For exact p no beta, use steepest. Multiplicative update: a_new = a * exp(-eta * H / grad_scale). Scale H by its max to set log changes order 0.1. Let scale = max(abs(H)) (or np.percentile). eta=0.2/scale.
   logf = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - eta*H
   avoid overflow clip logf to [-700, 700]
   a_new=exp(logf); norm; eval M.
   If M<best: accept, reset step? else maybe reduce eta and/or use a+a_new/2? We can do backtracking: try λ in [1,0.5,0.25,...] moving in multiplicative direction: a_trial = (a** (1-t) * a_exp** t)? def fast_autocorr(a):
 n=len(a); size=2*n-1; L=1 << (size-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:size]; due floating, small negative; maybe c=np.maximum(c,0)? The true b nonnegative. But for objective, np.convolve exact nonnegative. Small negative due roundoff can reduce max slightly if clipped? Max likely positive. Clipping negative to 0 avoids invalid. After irfft, roundoff ~1e-16. We can c[c<0 & >-1e-12]=0; but max unaffected. Do np.maximum(c,0) to be safe.
 return c.

       if Hmax>1e-15:
          for alpha in [1,0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05]:
             # direction d = -H (steepest descent for weighted M). Multiplicative update avoids negative:
             # candidate = normalize(a * exp(-alpha*H/Hmax))
             logc = np.log(a+1e-300) - alpha*H/Hmax
             # clip to avoid underflow; maybe floor: logc = np.maximum(logc, -50)
             c=np.exp(clip(logc,-50,50)); c=normalize(c)
             cb=auto(c); cM=cb.max()
             if cM < M*(1 - 1e-12):
                 a,c? accept, update b/M/best; break
          # if no accept, try interval interpolation in simplex: z=a - alpha*H/Hmax; project; same.
       # Pair moves:
       # Generate high/low relative to H
       high = top 30 by H; low = bottom 30 by H; include random bottom 20
       # Ensure source a>eps. select candidate pairs with positive H_i-H_j.
       # Maybe evaluate exact 20 best by approx.
       exact_improved=False
       for each candidate pair/frac top list:
          a_try = a copy; a_try[i]-=d; a_try[j]+=d; if negative continue; normalize (though sum preserved)
          tb=auto(a_try); tM=tb.max()
          if tM < M - 1e-14: accept, update; exact_improved=True; break
       if not exact_improved:
          # random exploratory move: use pressure or uniform
          if no_impr%100==...: add noise accept/reject anneal
       if improved: no_impr=0 else no_impr++
       if no_impr>1000: restart scale up?

Let's implement pressure function:
```
def pressure_from_peaks(peaks, weights, a):
    n=len(a)
    H=np.zeros(n)
    if len(peaks)<100:
       for kk,ww in zip(peaks,weights):
           k=int(kk); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
           if s<=e:
              H[s:e+1] += ww * a[k - np.arange(s,e+1)]
    else:
       p=np.zeros(2*n-1)
       p[peaks]=weights
       L=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(P*Ar,L)
       H=C[n-1:2*n-1]
    return H
```
Need ensure if using FFT, numerical small negative? H should nonnegative? weights positive, a nonnegative, so H≥0. Clip H=np.maximum(H,0). Good. Factor 2 outside.

But for large n, p zero allocation each time cost. Fine.

Approx function with current b and active top `appr_top` length up to 64:
```
def approx_move_max(b, a, i, j, d, keys):
    maxv=0
    for k in keys:
       val=b[k]
       if k-j in [0,n-1]: val += 2*d*a[k-j]
       if k-i in [0,n-1]: val -= 2*d*a[k-i]
       # quadratic
       q=0
       if k==2*i: q+=d*d
       if k==2*j: q+=d*d
       if k==i+j: q-=2*d*d
       val+=q
       if val>maxv: maxv=val
    # also new peaks at i+j,2i,2j if not in keys and b high? include
    for k in (i+j,2*i,2*j) if valid and not in key? evaluate exact with current b[k]
    return maxv
```

Now, exact candidate evaluation within local_search. Let's write robust functions:

`fast_autocorr(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
size=2*n-1
L=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:size]
return np.maximum(c,0.)
```
Optimization: If input already float64 contiguous. Use np.ascontiguousarray? okay.

`local_score_mul(a)`:
```
s=a.sum()
if s<0.01: inf
b=max(fast_autocorr(a))
return 2*len(a)*b/(s*s)
```
But local_search normalizes to sum1 so score=2nM.

`normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum()
if s<1e-15: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
a=a/s
# maybe clip min? no.
return a
```
For local_search, after normalize sum exactly 1 but due float maybe.

`blend(a1,a2,frac)`: normalize((1-frac)*a1+frac*a2).

`log_perturb(a, sigma, rng, eps=1e-15)`:
```
la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
la += sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
# keep some floor: la clipped [-40,40]
c=np.exp(np.clip(la,-40,40)); return normalize(c)
```
This may zero many if log<-40. Maybe use floor 1e-12: c = np.maximum(c,1e-12); then normalize. Good.

`active_selection(b, rng)`:
```
nbb=len(b); bmax=b.max()
tol = max(1e-12, bmax*5e-4)
band=np.nonzero(b >= bmax - tol)[0]
if len(band)>300:
   # random sample plus top 64 by value.
   # top order = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:64]
   # sample = rng.choice(band, 300, replace=False)
   # union unique, but top order may not all in band yes.
   idx = unique; if >300 sort by b descending and take 300? We want top focus.
else idx=band
# sort by b descending
idx=idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
if len(idx)>300: idx=idx[:300]
# weights: rank decay
ranks=np.arange(1,len(idx)+1)
w=1.0/ranks
# maybe also boost actual b differences: w *= 1+(b[idx]-b[idx][-1])/max(...)
w/=w.sum()

```
Top order via full argsort each loop O(n log n) with n=13399, maybe 1ms? Could be okay but loops many. We can use `np.argpartition` for top? For active, we already have b. Pressure: for len(idx)>80 use FFT. But p length dense zero each time. Need ensure L. We'll implement:
```
def pressure_general(peaks, weights, a):
 n=len(a)
 if len(peaks)<80:
   slices
 else:
   p=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   p[peaks]=weights
   L=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
   C=np.fft.irfft(P*Ar,L)
   H=C[n-1:2*n-1]
 H=np.maximum(H,0)
 return H
```


Let's write functions carefully.

`fast_autocorr` with possible input list:
```
def fast_autocorr(a):
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2*n - 1
    L = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:size]
    if c.size:
        # Clean tiny negatives
        c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c
```
This modifies copy; okay. But `c[c <0]` can be vector. For performance, use `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)`.

`evaluate_safe`:
```
def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        a=np.asarray(a,float)
        if a.ndim!=1: return inf
        a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0, posinf=1000, neginf=0)
        a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
        lst=a.tolist()
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except: return inf
```
But evaluate_sequence available. For final.

`local_score_fast(a)`:
```
s=a.sum(); n=len(a); if s<0.01: return inf
b=fast_autocorr(a); M=b.max() if b.size else 0
return 2.0*n*M/(s*s)
```
Fine.

`initial_candidates(parent, n_default)`:
```
def initial_candidates(parent, n_default):
  cands=[]
  if parent is not None:
     p=normalize(parent)
     n=len(p)
     cands.append(('parent',p))
  else:
     n=n_default
     p=make_left_sqrt(n)
     cands.append(('default',p))
  n_best = len(cands[0][1])
  # add others of same n
  left=make_left_sqrt(n_best)
  cands.append(('sqrt',left))
  # tilt: left * (1 - 0.15*i/n)
  x=np.arange(n_best,n_best+?)/n_best
  tilt1=normalize(left*(1-0.2*np.linspace(0,1,n_best)))
  cands.append(('tilt',tilt1))
  tilt2=normalize(left*(1+0.2*np.linspace(0,1,n_best)))
  cands.append(('tilt2',tilt2))
  # u-shape denom
  idx1=np.arange(1,n_best+1,dtype=float); idx2=n_best+1-idx1
  u=normalize(1.0/(np.sqrt(idx1)+np.sqrt(idx2)))
  cands.append(('u',u))
  # maybe parent jitter if parent
  rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
  if parent is not None:
     p=cands[0][1]
     for sig in [0.05,0.15]:
       loga=np.log(p+1e-12)+sig*rng.normal(0,1,n_best)
       c=np.exp(np.clip(loga,-40,40)); c=np.maximum(c,1e-12); c=normalize(c)
       cands.append(('jit%d'%int(sig*100),c))
  # evaluate and sort
  scored=[]
  for name,a in cands:
    sc=local_score_fast(a)
    if finite: scored.append((sc,name,a))
  sort; return scored
```

```
def select_active(b,rng):
    nbb=len(b)
    bmax=float(b.max())
    if nbb <= 64:
       idx=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
       w=np.ones(len(idx)); w/=w.sum(); return idx,w,bmax
    tol=max(1e-12, bmax*5e-4)
    topk=64
    top=np.argpartition(b, -topk)[-topk:]
    band=np.nonzero(b >= bmax - tol)[0]
    max_sample=256
    if len(band) > max_sample:
        samp = band[rng.integers(0, len(band), max_sample-topk, False)]? Need top include. Simpler use rng.permutation? But band could 10000; `choice` size min(max_sample, len(band)). Use max_sample = 256.
        samp = rng.choice(band, size=min(max_sample, len(band)), replace=False)
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, samp)))
    else:
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, band)))
    idx.sort()
    # Sort by b desc
    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
    if len(idx) > 256:
        idx = idx[:256]
    ranks=np.arange(1,len(idx)+1,dtype=float)
    w=1.0/ranks
    # add slight emphasis by relative b
    # If a peak just below M, rank lower; ok.
    w=w/w.sum()
    return idx,w,bmax
```

`pressure_from_peaks(peaks, weights, a)`:
```
def pressure_from_peaks(peaks, weights, a):
    n=len(a)
    H=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(peaks)==0: return H
    if len(peaks) < 100:
       for kk, ww in zip(peaks, weights):
          if ww == 0: continue
          k=int(kk); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
          if s<=e:
             H[s:e+1] += ww * a[k - np.arange(s,e+1)]
    else:
       p=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.float64)
       p[peaks]=weights
       L=1 << ((3*n-2)-1).bit_length()
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       Ar=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
       C=np.fft.irfft(P*Ar,L)
       H=C[n-1:2*n-1]
    H = np.maximum(H,0.0)
    return H
```
`k - np.arange` allocates; for 100 peaks okay. Could optimize: `a[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]`? Since k-idx from k-s down to k-e. Slices: `seg = a[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]`. This avoids arange. s=max(0,k-n+1), e=min(n-1,k). Need a[k-e : k-s+1] indices. Example k=0,s=0,e=0 -> a[0:1][::-1]=[a0]. k<n: s=0,e=k -> a[k-e: k-s+1]=a[0:k+1][::-1]. Good. k≥n-1: s=k-n+1,e=n-1 -> a[k-(n-1): k-(k-n+1)+1] = a[k-n+1 : n][::-1]. Good. Use this to speed.
```
H[s:e+1] += ww * a[k-e:k-s+1][::-1]
```
No temp except slice reversed view maybe copy? Slicing creates view, [::-1] view; addition works.

`approx_move_max` as top-level:
```
def approx_move_max(b,a,i,j,d,keys,n):
    maxv=0.0
    ii=int(i); jj=int(j); ii_plus_j=ii+jj; two_i=2*ii; two_j=2*jj
    size=2*n-1
    for kk in keys:
       k=int(kk)
       val=b[k]
       kj=k-jj
       if 0 <= kj < n:
          val += 2.0*d*a[kj]
       ki=k-ii
       if 0 <= ki < n:
          val -= 2.0*d*a[ki]
       q=0.0
       if k == two_i: q += d*d
       if k == two_j: q += d*d
       if k == ii_plus_j: q -= 2.0*d*d
       val += q
       if val > maxv: maxv=val
    # special k not necessarily in keys
    for k in (two_i, two_j, ii_plus_j):
       if 0 <= k < size:
          val=b[k]
          kj=k-jj
          if 0 <= kj < n: val += 2.0*d*a[kj]
          ki=k-ii
          if 0 <= ki < n: val -= 2.0*d*a[ki]
          q=0.0
          if k == two_i: q += d*d
          if k == two_j: q += d*d
          if k == ii_plus_j: q -= 2.0*d*d
          val += q
          if val > maxv: maxv=val
    return maxv
```
This may double count if special in keys, but max unaffected.

`log_perturb(a,sigma,rng)`:
```
la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
la += sigma*rng.standard_normal(len(a))
la=np.clip(la,-45,45)
c=np.exp(la)
c=np.maximum(c,1e-12)
return normalize(c)
```

`blend(a,b,frac)`: normalize.

`local_search(a_init, hard_deadline, seed, refs=None, verbose=True, min_time=?)`:
This is core. Let's outline with detailed variables.
```
def local_search(a_init, hard_deadline, seed, refs=None, verbose=False):
    n=len(a_init)
    a=normalize(a_init)
    b=fast_autocorr(a); M=float(b.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    score=2*n*M
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # If refs None: use [make_left_sqrt(n), maybe a_init copy]
    if refs is None: refs=[a.copy(), make_left_sqrt(n)]
    # Consider global reference sequence? We'll pass parent maybe.
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0:
        # active selection
        active,aw,bmax = select_active(b,rng)
        H=2.0*pressure_from_peaks(active,aw,a)
        improved_this_iter=False
        appr_keys = active[:64]
        # 1. multiplicative gradient candidates
        Hmax=float(H.max())
        if Hmax > 1e-15:
            for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0, 0.2, 4.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 3: break
                la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
                la -= scale*H/Hmax
                la=np.clip(la,-45,45)
                c=np.exp(la); c=np.maximum(c,1e-12); c=normalize(c)
                cb=fast_autocorr(c); cM=float(cb.max())
                if cM < M - 1e-15:
                    a=c; b=cb; M=cM; improved_this_iter=True
                    if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                       best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
                    break
        # 2. pair moves
        if not improved_this_iter:
            # Generate candidate pairs
            high_idx=np.argsort(H)[::-1][:60]
            low_idx=np.argsort(H)[:60]
            # Maybe add bottom half random? low_idx includes.
            cands=[]
            high_list=high_idx
            # To ensure sources not tiny mass, filter
            for i in high_list:
                ai=float(a[i])
                if ai <= 1e-12: continue
                hi=H[i]
                for j in low_idx:
                    if j == i: continue
                    hj=H[j]
                    if hj >= hi: continue
                    # priority
                    pri=(hi-hj)*min(ai,1.0)
                    cands.append((pri,int(i),int(j)))
            cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True) # lambda not allowed!
```

No lambda anywhere.

`interpolate_sequence` top-level:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
    old_n=len(f)
    if new_n <=0: return None
    if old_n == new_n: return f.copy()
    s=f.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    fn=f/s
    old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0,1,new_n)
    out=np.interp(new_x, old_x, fn)
    return normalize(out)
```

```
def local_search(a_init, hard_deadline, seed, refs, verbose):
    n=len(a_init)
    a=normalize(a_init)
    b=fast_autocorr(a); M=float(b.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    # refs ensure length n
    clean_refs=[]
    if refs:
       for r in refs:
          if r is not None and len(r)==n:
             clean_refs.append(normalize(r))
    if not clean_refs:
       clean_refs=[a.copy(), make_left_sqrt(n), make_u_shape(n)]
    # limit refs 3
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0:
       active,aw,bmax = select_active(b,rng)
       H=2.0*pressure_from_peaks(active,aw,a)
       improved=False
       appr_keys=active[:min(64,len(active))]
       Hmax=float(H.max())
       if Hmax > 1e-15:
          for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
              if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 3.0: break
              la=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
              la -= scale * H / Hmax
              la=np.clip(la, -45.0, 45.0)
              c=np.exp(la); c=np.maximum(c,1e-12); c=normalize(c)
              cb=fast_autocorr(c); cM=float(cb.max())
              if cM < M - 1e-15:
                  a=c; b=cb; M=cM; improved=True
                  if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                      best_M=M; best_a=a.copy()
                  break
       if not improved:
          # pair candidates
          high_idx=np.argsort(H)[::-1][:60]
          low_idx=np.argsort(H)[:60]
          cands=[]
          for i in high_idx:
              ii=int(i); ai=float(a[ii])
              if ai <= 1e-12: continue
              hi=H[ii]
              for j in low_idx:
                  jj=int(j)
                  if jj == ii: continue
                  hj=H[jj]
                  if hj >= hi: continue
                  pri=(hi-hj)*min(ai, 1.0)
                  cands.append((pri,ii,jj))
          cands.sort(reverse=True)
          top_pairs=[]; seen=set()
          for pri,ii,jj in cands:
              key=ii*1000000+JJ if n<100000? Use ii*10000000+jj
              if key not in seen:
                  seen.add(key); top_pairs.append((ii,jj))
                  if len(top_pairs)>=18: break
          exact_list=[]
          fracs=(0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1)
          for ii,jj in top_pairs:
              ai=float(a[ii])
              if ai <= 1e-12: continue
              for f in fracs:
                  d=ai*f
                  if d <= 1e-16: continue
                  am=approx_move_max(b,a,ii,jj,d,appr_keys,n)
                  exact_list.append((am,ii,jj,d))
                  if len(exact_list)>=60: break
              if len(exact_list)>=60: break
          # sort exact_list by am ascending
          exact_list.sort()
          accepted=False
          for am,ii,jj,d in exact_list:
              if am > M + 1e-14: break? If am > M, further approx not improve? Since approximate is max over subset; if approx > M, full cannot be <M? Actually approx could be under M if key subset max under M but a hidden peak >M? Hidden peaks before were below current M; but move could increase hidden peak above M. If am = max over subset, if am >= M, there is at least one monitored peak not reduced below current M. Full max could still be <M if monitored apex was overestimated? Approx formula exact for monitored peaks, so if monitored val >=M, full max >= that >=M, cannot improve. Good. So break if am > M - 1e-15 maybe; use if am > M: break. But due subset not including a peak that will exceed M, approx might <M while full fails; still exact checks. If approx >=M, definitely full cannot improve. So sort and break when am >= M -? Use if am >= M - 1e-14 break. But due floating, use if am > M - 1e-14: break.
              a_t=a.copy(); a_t[ii]-=d; a_t[jj]+=d
              st=a_t.sum()
              if st <=0: continue
              if st != 1.0: a_t=a_t/st
              bt=fast_autocorr(a_t); tM=float(bt.max())
              if tM < M - 1e-15:
                  a=a_t; b=bt; M=tM; improved=True; accepted=True
                  if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                     best_A=a.copy(); best_M=M
                  break
          if accepted: no_improve=0
          else:
             no_improve += 1
       if improved:
           if not accepted? For mult improved, set no_improve=0
       # If not improved by mult but pair maybe improved; above set.
```

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    max_time = min(float(budget_s), 1800.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, max_time - 80.0)
    parent=None
    try:
       parent_arr=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       parent_arr=np.where(np.isfinite(parent_arr), parent_arr, 0.0)
       parent_arr=np.clip(parent_arr,0,1000)
       if parent_arr.ndim !=1: parent_arr=parent_arr.reshape(-1)
       if parent_arr.size>0 and parent_arr.sum()>1e-6:
          parent=parent_arr
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 12000:
         parent=interpolate_sequence(parent, 10000)
    scored=initial_candidates(parent, 6700)
    if not scored:
         best_a=make_left_sqrt(6700); best_score=local_score_fast(best_a)
    else:
         best_a=scored[0][2]; best_score=scored[0][0]
    # exact best
    escore=evaluate_safe(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(escore): best_score=escore
    print initial
    refs=build_refs(best_a,parent)
    # initial search top candidates
    init_budget = min(180.0, (hard_deadline - time.monotonic())*0.15)
    if init_budget > 60.0:
        k=min(3,len(scored))
        per=init_budget/float(k)
        for idx, item in enumerate(scored[:k]):
            sc,name,a=item
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 60: break
            end = time.monotonic() + min(per, (hard_deadline-time.monotonic())*0.35)
            out_a,out_sc=local_search(a,end,seed=1000+idx,refs=refs,verbose=True)
            esc=evaluate_safe(out_a)
            if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score:
                best_score=esc; best_a=out_a
            elif np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score: # fallback if eval fails
                best_score=out_sc; best_a=out_a
            refs=build_refs(best_a,parent)
    # length exploration
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 240.0:
        n_current=len(best_a)
        n_exp=n_current+800
        if n_exp <= 10000 and n_exp > n_current:
            a_exp=interpolate_sequence(best_a,n_exp)
            end_exp=time.monotonic()+min(80.0,(hard_deadline-time.monotonic())*0.2)
            refs_exp=build_refs(a_exp,parent)
            out_a,out_sc=local_search(a_exp,end_exp,seed=2000,refs=refs_exp,verbose=True)
            esc=evaluate_safe(out_a)
            if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score - 1e-7:
                 best_score=esc; best_a=out_a; refs=build_refs(best_a,parent)
            elif np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score - 1e-7:
                 best_score=out_sc; best_a=out_a; refs=build_refs(best_a,parent)
    # final
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 30.0:
        out_a,out_sc=local_search(best_a,hard_deadline,seed=3000,refs=refs,verbose=True)
        esc=evaluate_safe(out_a)
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score:
            best_score=esc; best_a=out_a
        elif np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score:
            best_score=out_sc; best_a=out_a
    # final sanitize and return
    best_a=np.asarray(best_a,dtype=np.float64)
    best_a=np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a=normalize(best_a)
    final_score=evaluate_safe(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        # fallback
        n_fb=6700
        best_a=make_left_sqrt(n_fb)
        final_score=evaluate_safe(best_a)
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```

```
def initial_candidates(parent, default_n):
    cands=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
        p=normalize(parent)
        n=p.size
        cands.append(('parent',p))
    else:
        n=default_n
        p=make_left_sqrt(n)
        cands.append(('base',p))
    n=len(cands[0][1])
    left=make_left_sqrt(n)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cands.append(('sqrt', left))
    cands.append(('sqrt_dec', normalize(np.maximum(left*(1.0-0.25*x),1e-12))))
    cands.append(('sqrt_inc', normalize(np.maximum(left*(1.0+0.25*x),1e-12))))
    cands.append(('u', make_u_shape(n)))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(98765)
    if parent is not None:
       p=cands[0][1]
       cands.append(('jit1', log_perturb(p,0.05,rng)))
       cands.append(('jit2', log_perturb(p,0.15,rng)))
    scored=[]
    for name,a in cands:
       sc=local_score_fast(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((sc,name,a))
    scored.sort()
    return scored
```
`np.isfinite(sc)` returns bool if float. Good.
If `left*(1+0.25x)` at right x=1 ->1.25*left ~1.25/√n, okay.
`log_perturb` top-level uses `normalize` which uses floor? no. It uses np.maximum c 1e-12. Good.

`build_refs`:
```
def build_refs(base, parent):
    n=len(base)
    refs=[]
    if parent is not None:
       if parent.size != n: refs.append(interpolate_sequence(parent,n))
       else: refs.append(normalize(parent))
    refs.append(make_left_sqrt(n))
    refs.append(make_u_shape(n))
    refs.append(normalize(base))
    # maybe duplicates; limit unique? Not necessary.
    return refs[:5]
```
Could include base duplicate. Good.

Potential issue in local_search if refs contains arrays length n but not finite; normalize handles. Good.

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The code starts from the supplied construction if available, plus several
structured / randomized replacements.  It then repeatedly applies:
  - active-peak pressure estimates from the largest convolution terms,
  - steepest natural-gradient (multiplicative) steps,
  - exact pair mass-transfer moves ranked by an active-peak approximation,
  - occasional deterministic restarts / annealed moves to escape local minima.

All internal optimisation is done at L1-normalization (sum = 1), which is
valid because the objective is scale invariant.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def normalize(a):
    """Clip to [0, 1000] and rescale to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_autocorr(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution of a, computed by FFT."""
    a = np.ascontiguousarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if size <= 1:
        return a.astype(np.float64).copy()
    fft_len = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_len)[:size]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def local_score_fast(a):
    """Fast score using FFT instead of calling evaluate_sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = len(a)
    if s < 0.01 or n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (s * s))


def evaluate_safe(a):
    """Call the provided evaluator safely."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.ndim != 1:
            a = a.reshape(-1)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        return float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def make_left_sqrt(n, tilt=0.0):
    """One-sided singular initialized by 1/sqrt(i+1), optionally tilted."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx)
    if tilt != 0.0:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        a = a * (1.0 + tilt * x)
    return normalize(a)


def make_u_shape(n):
    """Symmetric smooth U-shape start: 1 / (sqrt(i) + sqrt(n+1-i))."""
    n = max(1, int(n))
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 / (np.sqrt(idx) + np.sqrt(n + 1.0 - idx))
    return normalize(a)


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    """Linearly interpolate a normalized sequence to a new length."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = len(f)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    fn = f / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, fn)
    return normalize(out)


def log_perturb(a, sigma, rng):
    """Perturb in log-space while preserving nonnegativity."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
    la += sigma * rng.standard_normal(len(a))
    la = np.clip(la, -45.0, 45.0)
    c = np.exp(la)
    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-12)
    return normalize(c)


def blend(a, b, frac):
    """Normalized convex blend of a and b."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    c = (1.0 - frac) * a + frac * b
    return normalize(c)


def select_active(b, rng):
    """
    Select a representative subset of peaks near the current maximum.

    If the maximum is flat over a large region, a random subset of that region
    is included so that the pressure is not biased to第一季few tied indices.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    nbb = len(b)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    if nbb <= 64:
        idx = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
        w = np.ones(len(idx), dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()
        return idx, w, bmax

    tol = max(1e-12, bmax * 5e-4)
    max_sample = 192
    topk = 64

    top = np.argpartition(b, -topk)[-topk:]
    band = np.nonzero(b >= bmax - tol)[0]

    if len(band) > max_sample:
        samp = band[rng.integers(0, len(band), max_sample, False)]
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, samp)))
    else:
        idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, band)))

    idx = idx[np.argsort(b[idx])[::-1]]
    if len(idx) > max_sample:
        idx = idx[:max_sample]

    ranks = np.arange(1, len(idx) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = 1.0 / ranks
    w /= w.sum()
    return idx, w, bmax


def pressure_from_peaks(peaks, weights, a):
    """
    Compute for every coordinate j:
        sum_k weights[k] * a[k-j]
    with a[t]=0 outside [0,n-1].  This is the subgradient building block for
    active convolution peaks.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    H = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(peaks) == 0:
        return H

    if len(peaks) < 100:
        for kk, ww in zip(peaks, weights):
            if ww == 0.0:
                continue
            k = int(kk)
            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            e = min(n - 1, k)
            if s <= e:
                H[s:e + 1] += ww * a[k - e:k - s + 1][::-1]
    else:
        p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        p[peaks] = weights
        max_len = 3 * n - 2
        fft_len = 1 << (max_len - 1).bit_length()
        P = np.fft.rfft(p, fft_len)
        Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], fft_len)
        C = np.fft.irfft(P * Ar, fft_len)
        H = C[n - 1:2 * n - 1]

    return np.maximum(H, 0.0)


def approx_move_max(b, a, i, j, d, keys, n):
    """
    Approximate the new peak after moving mass d from i to j, using only a
    subset of currently relevant peaks plus the few directly affected positions.
    """
    ii = int(i)
    jj = int(j)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    two_i = 2 * ii
    two_j = 2 * jj
    ij = ii + jj
    d2 = d * d
    md = 2.0 * d

    maxv = 0.0

    for kk in keys:
        k = int(kk)
        val = float(b[k])

        kj = k - jj
        if 0 <= kj < n:
            val += md * float(a[kj])
        ki = k - ii
        if 0 <= ki < n:
            val -= md * float(a[ki])

        q = 0.0
        if k == two_i:
            q += d2
        if k == two_j:
            q += d2
        if k == ij:
            q -= 2.0 * d2
        val += q

        if val > maxv:
            maxv = val

    for k in (two_i, two_j, ij):
        if 0 <= k < size:
            val = float(b[k])

            kj = k - jj
            if 0 <= kj < n:
                val += md * float(a[kj])
            ki = k - ii
            if 0 <= ki < n:
                val -= md * float(a[ki])

            q = 0.0
            if k == two_i:
                q += d2
            if k == two_j:
                q += d2
            if k == ij:
                q -= 2.0 * d2
            val += q

            if val > maxv:
                maxv = val

    return maxv


def initial_candidates(parent, default_n):
    """Build a portfolio of good candidate starts and score them fast."""
    cands = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        p = normalize(parent)
        n = len(p)
        cands.append(("parent", p))
    else:
        n = int(default_n)
        p = make_left_sqrt(n)
        cands.append(("base", p))

    n = len(cands[0][1])
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    left = make_left_sqrt(n)
    cands.append(("sqrt", left))
    cands.append(("sqrt_dec", normalize(np.maximum(left * (1.0 - 0.25 * x), 1e-12))))
    cands.append(("sqrt_inc", normalize(np.maximum(left * (1.0 + 0.25 * x), 1e-12))))
    cands.append(("u", make_u_shape(n)))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)
    if parent is not None:
        p = cands[0][1]
        cands.append(("jit1", log_perturb(p, 0.05, rng)))
        cands.append(("jit2", log_perturb(p, 0.15, rng)))

    scored = []
    for name, a in cands:
        sc = local_score_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, name, a))

    scored.sort()
    return scored


def build_refs(base, parent):
    """Reference sequences for restarts, all with the current length."""
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(base)
    refs = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size == n:
            refs.append(normalize(parent))
        else:
            refs.append(interpolate_sequence(parent, n))

    refs.append(make_left_sqrt(n))
    refs.append(make_u_shape(n))
    refs.append(normalize(base))

    return refs[:5]


def local_search(a_init, hard_deadline, seed, refs, verbose=False):
    """
    Refine one sequence by alternating natural-gradient steps and exact
    pair-mass-transfer moves.  Returns (best_array, best_fast_score).
    """
    n = len(a_init)
    a = normalize(a_init)
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    M = float(b.max())

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    no_improve = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    clean_refs = []
    if refs is not None:
        for r in refs:
            if r is None:
                continue
            r = np.asarray(r, dtype=np.float64)
            if len(r) == n:
                clean_refs.append(normalize(r))
    if not clean_refs:
        clean_refs = [a.copy(), make_left_sqrt(n), make_u_shape(n)]
    if len(clean_refs) > 5:
        clean_refs = clean_refs[:5]

    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 2.0:
        active, aw, bmax = select_active(b, rng)
        H = 2.0 * pressure_from_peaks(active, aw, a)
        appr_keys = active[:min(64, len(active))]
        Hmax = float(H.max())
        improved = False

        # --- Natural-gradient / multiplicative descent attempt ---
        if Hmax > 1e-15:
            for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 3.0:
                    break
                la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-15))
                la -= scale * H / Hmax
                la = np.clip(la, -45.0, 45.0)
                c = np.exp(la)
                c = np.maximum(c, 1e-12)
                c = normalize(c)

                cb = fast_autocorr(c)
                cM = float(cb.max())
                if cM < M - 1e-15:
                    a = c
                    b = cb
                    M = cM
                    improved = True
                    if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    break

        # --- Exact local pair mass-transfer moves ---
        if not improved:
            if n > 60:
                high_idx = np.argpartition(H, -60)[-60:]
                low_idx = np.argpartition(H, 60)[:60]
            else:
                order = np.argsort(H)
                high_idx = order[::-1]
                low_idx = order

            cands = []
            for i in high_idx:
                ii = int(i)
                ai = float(a[ii])
                if ai <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                hi = float(H[ii])
                for j in low_idx:
                    jj = int(j)
                    if jj == ii:
                        continue
                    hj = float(H[jj])
                    if hj >= hi:
                        continue
                    pri = (hi - hj) * min(ai, 1.0)
                    cands.append((pri, ii, jj))

            cands.sort(reverse=True)

            top_pairs = []
            seen = set()
            for pri, ii, jj in cands:
                key = ii * 10000000 + jj
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    top_pairs.append((ii, jj))
                    if len(top_pairs) >= 18:
                        break

            exact_list = []
            fracs = (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1)
            for ii, jj in top_pairs:
                ai = float(a[ii])
                if ai <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                for f in fracs:
                    d = ai * f
                    if d <= 1e-16:
                        continue
                    am = approx_move_max(b, a, ii, jj, d, appr_keys, n)
                    if am < M + 1e-13:
                        exact_list.append((am, ii, jj, d))
                        if len(exact_list) >= 60:
                            break
                if len(exact_list) >= 60:
                    break

            if not exact_list and top_pairs:
                ii, jj = top_pairs[0]
                exact_list.append((M, ii, jj, float(a[ii]) * 0.05))

            exact_list.sort()

            for am, ii, jj, d in exact_list:
                if am > M - 1e-14:
                    break
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 2.0:
                    break

                a_t = a.copy()
                a_t[ii] -= d
                a_t[jj] += d
                if a_t[ii] < 0.0:
                    continue
                st = float(a_t.sum())
                if st <= 0.0:
                    continue
                if st != 1.0:
                    a_t = a_t / st

                bt = fast_autocorr(a_t)
                tM = float(bt.max())
                if tM < M - 1e-15:
                    a = a_t
                    b = bt
                    M = tM
                    improved = True
                    if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

            # --- Mild annealed/random moves when stalled ---
            if no_improve > 80 and (no_improve == 100 or no_improve % 150 == 0):
                cur_score = 2.0 * n * M
                T = 1.0e-4 * (1.0 + min(1.0, no_improve / 1000.0))

                pool = []
                for sig in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
                    pool.append(log_perturb(a, sig, rng))
                for ref in clean_refs[:3]:
                    for frac in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
                        pool.append(blend(a, ref, frac))

                for c in pool:
                    cb = fast_autocorr(c)
                    cM = float(cb.max())
                    cs = 2.0 * n * cM
                    delta = cs - cur_score
                    if cs < cur_score - 1e-13:
                        a = c
                        b = cb
                        M = cM
                        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        break
                    elif delta < 6.0 * T and rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / T):
                        a = c
                        b = cb
                        M = cM
                        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                            best_M = M
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        break

            # --- Deterministic restart near global best when very stuck ---
            if no_improve >= 250 and (no_improve - 250) % 150 == 0:
                global_fast = 2.0 * n * best_M
                current_fast = 2.0 * n * M
                threshold = max(global_fast, current_fast) + 0.002

                pool_a = None
                pool_b = None
                pool_M = None
                pool_score = threshold

                for sig in (0.05, 0.15, 0.30):
                    c = log_perturb(a, sig, rng)
                    cb = fast_autocorr(c)
                    cM = float(cb.max())
                    cs = 2.0 * n * cM
                    if cs < pool_score:
                        pool_score = cs
                        pool_a = c
                        pool_b = cb
                        pool_M = cM

                for ref in clean_refs[:4]:
                    for frac in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
                        c = blend(a, ref, frac)
                        cb = fast_autocorr(c)
                        cM = float(cb.max())
                        cs = 2.0 * n * cM
                        if cs < pool_score:
                            pool_score = cs
                            pool_a = c
                            pool_b = cb
                            pool_M = cM

                if pool_a is not None:
                    a = pool_a
                    b = pool_b
                    M = pool_M
                    no_improve = 0
                    if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                        best_M = M
                        best_a = a.copy()

            # --- Prevent drifting too far below the known best ---
            score_gap = 2.0 * n * (M - best_M)
            if score_gap > 0.002:
                a = best_a.copy()
                b = fast_autocorr(a)
                M = best_M
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        if verbose:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_print > 25.0:
                print(
                    "local n=%d best_fast=%.9f current_fast=%.9f no_impr=%d t=%.1fs"
                    % (n, 2.0 * n * best_M, 2.0 * n * M, no_improve, now - t0)
                )
                last_print = now

    return best_a.copy(), float(2.0 * n * best_M)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    """
    Generate the best sequence found within the time budget.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    max_time = min(float(budget_s), 1800.0)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, max_time - 80.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1:
            p = p.reshape(-1)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-6:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 12000:
        parent = interpolate_sequence(parent, 10000)

    scored = initial_candidates(parent, 6700)
    if not scored:
        best_a = make_left_sqrt(6700)
        best_score = local_score_fast(best_a)
    else:
        best_a = scored[0][2]
        best_score = scored[0][0]

    escore = evaluate_safe(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(escore):
        best_score = escore

    print("initial best: score=%.9f n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    refs = build_refs(best_a, parent)

    # Short portfolio polish to avoid following only one basin.
    init_budget = min(240.0, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.16)
    if init_budget > 60.0:
        k = min(6, len(scored))
        per = init_budget / float(max(1, k))
        if per < 30.0:
            k = min(3, len(scored))
            per = init_budget / float(max(1, k))

        for idx in range(k):
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 60.0:
                break
            sc, name, a = scored[idx]
            end = time.monotonic() + min(per, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35)
            if end <= time.monotonic():
                continue
            out_a, out_sc = local_search(a, end, seed=1000 + idx, refs=refs, verbose=True)

            esc = evaluate_safe(out_a)
            if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score:
                best_score = esc
                best_a = out_a
            elif np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score:
                best_score = out_sc
                best_a = out_a

            refs = build_refs(best_a, parent)
            print("after initial %s: best_score=%.9f n=%d" % (name, best_score, len(best_a)))

    # Optional length exploration.  Slightly longer sequences can reduce finite-n effects,
    # but we limit this to keep the runtime predictable.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 240.0:
        n_now = len(best_a)
        n_exp = n_now + 800
        if n_exp <= 10000 and n_exp > n_now:
            a_exp = interpolate_sequence(best_a, n_exp)
            end_exp = time.monotonic() + min(80.0, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.2)
            if end_exp > time.monotonic():
                refs_exp = build_refs(a_exp, parent)
                out_a, out_sc = local_search(a_exp, end_exp, seed=2000, refs=refs_exp, verbose=True)
                esc = evaluate_safe(out_a)

                if (np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score - 1e-7) or (
                        not np.isfinite(esc) and np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score - 1e-7):
                    if np.isfinite(esc):
                        best_score = esc
                    else:
                        best_score = out_sc
                    best_a = out_a
                    refs = build_refs(best_a, parent)
                    print("length exploration improved: score=%.9f n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    # Final deep polish on the current best.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 30.0:
        out_a, out_sc = local_search(best_a, hard_deadline, seed=3000, refs=refs, verbose=True)
        esc = evaluate_safe(out_a)
        if np.isfinite(esc) and esc < best_score:
            best_score = esc
            best_a = out_a
        elif np.isfinite(out_sc) and out_sc < best_score:
            best_score = out_sc
            best_a = out_a

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = normalize(best_a)

    final_score = evaluate_safe(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        best_a = make_left_sqrt(6700)
        final_score = evaluate_safe(best_a)

    print("FINAL returned score=%.9f n=%d" % (final_score, len(best_a)))
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
